### [1] [Edge-cusped compactons from gradient-power nonlinear dispersion: exact transitions and singular spectral structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00552)
*Francisco R. Villatoro*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该文引入一个双指数守恒色散族（非线性通量指数p、梯度色散指数r），将紧致化、自由边界正则性与线性化谱结构机制分离，同时保留显式行波理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望在单一退化色散模型中同时研究紧致子的几何奇异性、弱可容许性、谱结构与数值正则性，因此构建一个保留显式行波解的可调双指数族。

Method: 构造满足守恒律的双指数色散族（r=1时退化为绝对值Rosenau-Hyman方程）；分析行波解、振幅-速度律与宽度关系；推导两条相变曲线pr=1与r(p-1)=2；对r≥1的线性化用奇异Sturm-Liouville算子的Friedrichs实现，并作紧致子自适应的变量替换化为Jacobi-超几何型特征值方程；结合傅里叶尾谱、投影误差与谱配置法进行数值实验。

Result: 变形不改变振幅-速度律，但使宽度依赖振幅（r=1除外）；pr=1区分无限尾孤立波与紧致子，r(p-1)=2区分自由边界正则的紧致子与斜率发散的边缘尖角紧致子，后者仍为分布解且无奇异测度；谱给出两个显式序列、恰一个负特征值与一维平移核；数值测得的傅里叶尾与投影误差速率与自由边界指数预测一致，谱配置时间积分可相干传播尖角波（相位漂移小），截断点谱未检测到实部。

Conclusion: 该族提供了一个统一的显式检验平台，可在同一退化色散模型中研究紧致子的几何奇异、弱可容许、谱结构与数值正则性。

Abstract: We introduce a conservative two-exponent dispersive family, with $p>1$ the nonlinear-flux exponent and $r>0$ the gradient-dispersion exponent, that separates the mechanisms governing compactification, free-boundary regularity and linearised spectral structure while retaining an explicit travelling-wave theory. The family reduces to the absolute Rosenau-Hyman equation when $r=1$. The deformation leaves the amplitude-speed law unchanged, but makes the width amplitude dependent except at $r=1$. Two independent transition curves emerge: $pr=1$ separates infinite-tail solitary waves from compactons, whereas $r(p-1)=2$ separates compactons with a regular free boundary from edge-cusped compactons with divergent slope. The latter remain distributional solutions, with no singular measures at either the free boundary or the crest. For $r\ge1$, the linearisation factors through a singular Sturm-Liouville operator whose natural Friedrichs realisation is solvable: after a compacton-adapted change of variable, the eigenvalue equation is of Jacobi-hypergeometric type, with two explicit spectral sequences, exactly one negative eigenvalue and a one-dimensional translation kernel. Numerical experiments connect these exact structures to approximation and dynamics. Measured Fourier tails and projection errors agree with the rates predicted by the free-boundary exponent, while padded Fourier-Galerkin time integration propagates the cusped waves coherently with small phase drift. A structure-adapted Jacobi-Galerkin diagnostic of the full generator finds no detectable real part in the tested truncated point spectra. The family therefore provides an explicit testbed in which geometric singularity, weak admissibility, spectral structure and numerical regularity of compactons can be studied within a single degenerate dispersive model.

</details>


### [2] [Discrete PT-symmetric solitons on branched lattices: Vertex states, stability, and vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00774)
*M. Akramov,A. Fayzullaev,O. Tojakhmadova,T. Akhmadjanov*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 研究了PT对称四臂星形图中的离散非线性薛定谔方程定域态，发现分叉几何支撑的离散涡旋态及其稳定性窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分叉结构引入了格点维度自由度，会从根本上改变非线性定域行为，这种效应在一维晶格中不存在，因此需要研究分叉几何对新奇定域态（尤其是拓扑类态）的调控作用。

Method: 在PT对称四臂星形图上建立离散非线性薛定谔方程模型，从反连续极限出发用延拓法构造离散涡旋态，考察增益-损耗平衡下的演化，并讨论其电路实现方案。

Result: 识别出具有固定拓扑荷的离散涡旋态；该态在增益-损耗平衡下演化为携带电流的态，并展现出常规分支所不具备的稳定性窗口。

Conclusion: 分叉几何可支撑新的拓扑定域涡旋态，并在PT对称条件下提供额外的稳定性区域，同时具备电气路实现的可能性。

Abstract: Branching fundamentally modifies nonlinear localization by introducing vertex degrees of freedom absent in one-dimensional lattices. We investigate localized states in a PT-symmetric four-edge star graph described by the discrete nonlinear Schrödinger equation. We identify a discrete vortex state with a fixed topological charge, supported by the branching geometry. Constructed by continuation from the anticontinuum limit, the vortex evolves into a current-carrying state in the presence of balanced gain and loss and exhibits stability windows absent for the conventional branches. A possible electric-circuit realization of the proposed model is discussed.

</details>


### [3] [The role of root allometry and root overlap in vegetation pattern formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01730)
*Jelle van der Voort,Ricardo Martinez-Garcia,Arjen Doelman*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该文研究根系异速生长与根系重叠导致的地下竞争如何影响非局部标量植被模型中的图灵斑图形成，揭示根系重叠带来的双积分结构与两种失稳机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部植被模型中常用单一卷积项刻画资源竞争，忽略了根系异速生长与根系重叠的生态作用，需明确这些根系特征对斑图形成条件的影响。

Method: 从空间显式的植被-水双组分系统出发，根系异速生长由随地上生物量密度增大而扩展的相互作用核表示；在快水动力学极限下消去水变量，得到非局部标量植被动力学模型，并分析其图灵不稳定性机制。

Result: 标量模型出现双积分结构而非单卷积项；识别出两种图灵失稳机制：一是根系增长需快于死亡，二是竞争压力区的形成；根系异速生长促进两种机制，而根系重叠抑制竞争压力区的出现。

Conclusion: 根系异速生长与根系重叠均是研究植被斑图形成时不可忽略的关键特征。

Abstract: We investigate how root allometry and below-ground root competition due to root overlap affect the conditions for pattern formation in non-local scalar vegetation models. We start from a spatially explicit two-component vegetation-water system in which plants acquire soil water through their laterally extended root systems, with root allometry represented by an interaction kernel whose spatial extent increases with the above-ground biomass density. In the limit of fast water dynamics, we eliminate the water variable, resulting in a non-local scalar model for the vegetation dynamics. This reduction reveals that accounting for root overlap naturally leads to a double integral structure in the scalar model, in contrast to the single convolution term commonly used to model non-local competition for resources. For the resulting scalar model, we identify two distinct mechanisms that can drive Turing instabilities. The first instability mechanism requires root growth to outpace mortality, while the second mechanism relies on the development of competitive pressure zones, in which vegetation growth is impeded by the cumulative competitive pressure exerted by neighbouring areas with higher biomass density. Root allometry promotes the occurrence of both instability mechanisms, while root overlap inhibits the emergence of competitive pressure zones. Hence, our results show that both root allometry and root overlap are important features to consider when studying vegetation pattern formation.

</details>


### [4] [Stability and instability analysis of resonance-induced nonlinear bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01875)
*Jackson C. Turner,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究具有紧支撑位势的聚焦型一维三次非线性薛定谔/GP方程，针对由线性算子反射系数的纯虚零点（透射共振）或下半平面纯虚极点（散射共振）所诱发的非线性束缚态分支，建立了其在分岔点附近轨道稳定性与不稳定性的精确判据。推论表明：当共振频率足够小且共振本征模严格正时，共振诱导非线性态是轨道稳定的。数值模拟进一步探索了理论未覆盖的区间及长时间动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（turner2026resonance）证明了线性薛定谔算子反射系数的共振（透射共振或散射共振）可诱导出非线性束缚态分支，且这些态在严格正的L²激发阈值处分岔，区别于从点谱（上半平面极点）在零L²范数处分岔的常规非线性束缚态。然而，这些共振诱导态的稳定性性质尚缺乏精确刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白，给出其非线性轨道稳定性/不稳定性的判据。

Method: 针对聚焦型一维三次NLS-GP方程，其中线性算子H_V=−∂_x²+V(x)的位势V紧支撑。基于分岔理论，在分岔点附近对共振诱导非线性束缚态进行轨道稳定性分析，建立稳定性与不稳定性的精确判据；通过分析共振频率与线性共振本征模符号等量，推导稳定性条件；并结合数值模拟研究理论范围之外的参数区间及稳定/不稳定共振诱导态附近的长时间动力学。

Result: 建立了共振诱导非线性态在分岔点附近轨道稳定性与不稳定性的精确判据。得到一个推论：若i) 底层线性（散射或透射）共振频率足够小，且ii) 对应线性共振本征模严格为正，则共振诱导非线性态是轨道稳定的。数值模拟揭示了理论无法覆盖的参数区间中的行为，并展示了在稳定与不稳定共振诱导非线性束缚态附近初始条件的长时间动力学演化。

Conclusion: 本文为共振诱导非线性束缚态的稳定性提供了精确的数学判据，明确了共振频率大小与共振本征模符号对轨道稳定性的决定性作用，从而将线性共振特性与非线性动力学稳定性直接联系起来。数值实验补充验证并拓展了理论结果，揭示了长时间演化特征。

Abstract: We study the focusing one-dimensional cubic nonlinear Schrödinger / Gross--Pitaevskii equation (NLS-GP), where the potential of the underlying linear Schrödinger operator, $H_V=-\partial_x^2+V(x)$, is compactly supported. In \cite{turner2026resonance} the authors proved that purely imaginary zeros (transmission resonances) or purely imaginary poles in the lower half plane (scattering resonances) of a reflection coefficient of $H_V$ seed branches of {\it resonance-induced nonlinear bound states}, and that these states bifurcate at a strictly positive $L^2(\mathbb R)$ excitation threshold. This is in contrast to nonlinear bound states which bifurcate at zero $L^2$-norm from point spectra of $H_V$ (corresponding to poles in the upper half plane). In this paper we establish precise criteria for the nonlinear orbital stability and instability of the resonance-induced states near the bifurcation point. A corollary is that resonance-induced nonlinear states are orbitally stable if i) the underlying linear (scattering or transmission) resonance frequency is sufficiently small, and ii) the corresponding linear resonance eigenmode is strictly positive. Numerical simulations are presented to explore regimes not accessible to our theory, and to explore the large time dynamics for initial conditions near stable and unstable resonance-induced nonlinear bound states.

</details>

### [5] [Beyond Lie Algebras: Lie-Wedge Stratification and Pure-State Stabilizability in Single-Channel Qubit Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00046)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无漂移单量子位系统的控制能力，证明其由生成代数与Lie楔几何划分为十三个结构层，并借此判定楔中是否存在具有全局吸引纯态的唯一生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lie代数刻画控制生成元的组合，但在开放系统中会抹去可逆控制与不可逆耗散之间的区分；Lie楔能保留这一信息，因此需要以Lie楔为工具重新刻画开放量子系统的控制能力。

Method: 针对无漂移单量子位系统，允许双向哈密顿控制与至多一个由无迹跳跃算符独立驱动的耗散通道；用生成代数与其Lie楔几何进行分类，并以控制代数及四个通道性质确定所属层。

Result: 证明这类系统落入十三个结构层；每一楔中可逆方向恰为相干控制方向；利用分层判定楔是否包含具有唯一全局吸引纯态的生成元：五个层中可稳定化性依赖额外通道数据；在一个例外层中，任何单一控制旋转的耗散方向均不足，但对楔中两个此类方向取平均可实现稳定化。

Conclusion: Lie楔揭示了生成Lie代数所遗漏的控制能力，说明在开放量子控制中区分可逆与不可逆生成元对稳定化判定至关重要。

Abstract: Lie algebras describe how control generators combine, but in open systems they erase the distinction between reversible control and irreversible dissipation. Lie wedges retain this information. We study drift-free single-qubit systems with bidirectional Hamiltonian controls and at most one independently actuated dissipator generated by a traceless jump operator. In this work, we prove that they fall into thirteen structural strata defined by the generated algebra and its Lie-wedge geometry. The control algebra and four channel properties determine the stratum; in each wedge, the reversible directions are precisely the coherent controls. We then use the stratification to determine whether a wedge contains a generator with a unique globally attractive pure state. In five strata, stabilizability depends on additional channel data. In one exceptional stratum, no single control-rotated dissipative direction suffices, but averaging two such directions in the wedge yields stabilization. Lie wedges therefore reveal control capabilities that the generated Lie algebra misses.

</details>


### [6] [Sharp Target-Domain Certificates for Quantum-Kernel Advantage under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00080)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Asier González-Santocildes,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文在无额外假设下给出了有限批次"量子预测优势"的锐利识别区间：零标签上界对115个经典核在八类偏移下为0.002–0.088；回顾性审计用0–33/500个标签即可将上界压至0.010；两项前瞻任务满足预设判据（20个效应全为负），第三项未通过特征门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子预测优势的评估通常依赖已知目标标签，缺少在标签未审计情形下的无假设推断手段，导致协议有效性与量子相关性难以区分。

Method: 对固定候选相对于预设固定经典核族最优成员的有限批次优势，在任意有界损失、未审计标签任意补全下推导无假设的锐利识别区间；0-1准确率给出精确闭式部分标签更新；在八个安全偏移上比较零标签上界与115个经典核，并做回顾性审计与两任务前瞻验证及协议对照。

Result: 八个偏移下零标签上界跨0.002–0.088；回顾性审计将各上界降至0.010，仅需0–33/500个标签（含纠缠-ZZ模型）。前瞻验证中四项任务-分类器中位数需0–3个标签（总体中位数0，最大76），20个实现效应全为负，第三任务未过特征门；协议对照可反转表面优势，有限次噪声可提升预测区分度但不带来有效优势。

Conclusion: 该框架可将协议有效性、目标预测不可替代性与量子相关性三者分离评估，为量子预测优势提供无假设、标签高效的审计方法。

Abstract: Quantum predictive advantage under shift usually requires known target labels. We derive the assumption-free sharp identified interval for the finite-batch advantage of a fixed candidate over the best member of a prespecified fixed classical-kernel family under any bounded loss and unrestricted completions of the unaudited labels; zero-one accuracy has exact closed-form partial-label updates. Across eight security shifts, zero-label upper endpoints against 115 classical kernels span 0.002-0.088. Retrospective auditing reduces every endpoint to 0.010 with 0-33 of 500 labels, including entangling-ZZ models. Prospective corroboration on two eligible tasks met predefined criteria: four task-classifier medians require 0-3 labels (overall median 0; maximum 76), and all 20 realized effects are negative; a third task fails its feature gate. Protocol controls reverse an apparent advantage, while finite-shot noise can increase predictive distinctness without useful advantage. The framework separates protocol validity, target predictive indispensability, and quantum relevance.

</details>


### [7] [Exact Posterior Prediction from Product Haar Measurements and a Randomized-Mesh Maximum-Likelihood Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00117)
*Naqueeb Ahmad Warsi,Ayanava Dasgupta,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究未知有限维纯量子态单个未测量副本的预测：在单副本Haar POVM下获得可分离观测，其相对熵损失下的Bayes预测态为满秩后验均值，可用Gram矩阵子式永久式精确表示，并证明极小极大性；给出有限样本后验纯度界与相对熵保证（样本量 O((d²/ε)log(d/ε))），并通过随机定向有限射影网格与选择性MLE构造正则有限字母实验，嵌套细化网格可恢复精细Fisher信息，得到渐进尺度 (d/ε)log(d/ε)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在仅观测到若干副本、需预测另一未测量副本的情境下，如何对未知有限维纯量子态进行预测，并在相对熵损失下建立精确可算的预测态与有限样本性能保证。

Method: 采用单副本Haar POVM获得固定可分离观测；计算满秩后验均值（基于结果的Gram矩阵子式永久式）作为Bayes预测态，并证明其正性与归一化及在这些固定结果下决策规则的极小极大性。利用后验均值的Hilbert–Schmidt投影与精确分析的线性反演竞争者，证明有限样本后验纯度界与相对熵保证。为连接细乘积数据与正则射线值实验，随机抽取一个有限射影网格方向并在整个样本中固定，采用选定的有限字母MLE；通过嵌套恢复网格恢复精细Fisher信息。

Result: 得到后验均值永久式表示的精确求值与性质证明；证明其在固定结果决策规则中的极小极大性；给出样本量 O((d²/ε)log(d/ε)) 的相对熵保证与后验纯度界；证明每个足够细的固定网格给出正则有限字母模型，其选定MLE具有Haar平均逆Fisher系数 \overline{a}_k，细化网格后 \overline{a}_k→d-1；由此得到精细后验的ε-最优前导相对熵与后验重叠界，以及更紧的固定维渐进尺度 (d/ε)log(d/ε)（非均匀有限样本保证）。

Conclusion: 在固定可分离观测下，满秩后验均值可作为精确可算且极小极大的Bayes预测态；通过随机定向有限射影网格与选定MLE，可在不依赖确定性有限样本唯一性或三角网格调度的前提下建立正则有限字母模型，并获得精细后验的ε-最优前导相对熵与后验重叠界及渐进尺度 (d/ε)log(d/ε)。

Abstract: We study prediction of one unmeasured copy of an unknown finite-dimensional pure quantum state after independently measuring the observed copies with the one-copy Haar POVM. For the resulting fixed separable observation, the Bayes predictive state under quantum relative-entropy loss is the full-rank posterior mean. We evaluate it exactly through permanents of minors of the outcome Gram matrix, prove positivity and normalization of the permanent representation, and establish minimaxity among decision rules based on these fixed outcomes. Using Hilbert--Schmidt projection of the posterior mean and an exactly analyzed linear-inversion competitor, we prove a finite-sample posterior-purity bound and an explicit relative-entropy guarantee with sample count $O((d^2/ε)\log(d/ε))$. Using the exact collective benchmarks proved in a companion paper, we also obtain entropy and posterior-purity comparisons. To connect the fine product data to a regular ray-valued experiment without assuming deterministic finite-sample uniqueness, we draw one random orientation for a finite projective mesh, retain it for the entire sample, and apply a selected finite-alphabet MLE in the resulting reference experiment. A nested recovering mesh uniformly recovers the fine Fisher information. Consequently every sufficiently fine fixed mesh gives a regular finite-alphabet model. Its selected MLE has Haar-averaged inverse-Fisher coefficient $\overline a_k$, and refining the mesh after the fixed-mesh large-sample limit makes $\overline a_k$ approach $d-1$. This yields epsilon-optimal leading relative-entropy and posterior-overlap bounds for the fine posterior and the sharper fixed-dimensional asymptotic scale $(d/ε)\log(d/ε)$. The latter is not asserted as a uniform finite-sample guarantee. No deterministic finite-sample MLE uniqueness or triangular mesh schedule is used.

</details>


### [8] [Global and nonlocal magic of quantum many-body scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00129)
*Lukas Ebner,Giovanni Cataldi,Kaidi Xu,Jad C. Halimeh,Emanuele Tirrito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用全局与非局域非稳定化性（magic）度量，研究(1+1)维阿贝尔Z2与U(1)格点规范理论中有限温度附近的量子多体疤痕，推导出仅依赖Schmidt谱的非局域迹距离magic的闭式表达式，揭示疤痕本征态在低纠缠下仍具有广泛全局magic和异常大的非局域magic。

Abstract: Entanglement cost is the asymptotic rate of Bell pairs required to prepare a quantum state. Its regularized definition has made exact evaluation difficult, even for isotropic states (i.e., depolarized maximally entangled states). In this work, we determine the entanglement cost of every qubit isotropic state and, more generally, every two-qubit Bell-diagonal state. Our proof uses a family of suitably tuned qubit semigroups to transform a general log-Sobolev entropy bound into supporting lines of Wootters' function. A recent exact tensorization theorem of Dong et al. [arXiv:2606.17729] extends these bounds to arbitrarily many copies, yielding a general lower bound on entanglement cost. Matching this bound with the entanglement of formation also determines the exact cost for a broader class of two-qubit states. For qudit isotropic states, we derive another general lower bound from the full depolarizing $2\to 3$ norm. This bound substantially improves on the PPT-relative entropy of entanglement and nearly matches the entanglement of formation, leaving a gap of at most $2\%$ of $\log d$ that vanishes as $d$ grows. Finally, the exact qubit results and qudit bounds extend to the entanglement cost of preparing quantum depolarizing channels under both parallel and sequential strategies.

</details>

Abstract: Parameterized quantum circuits require repeated measurements to estimate objective gradients, making the number of circuit evaluations a central resource. Simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (SPSA) is widely used because it obtains a full gradient estimate from only two cost evaluations, independently of the number of parameters. We analyze the random perturbation for the structured Fourier landscapes generated by quantum circuits. For a finite update width, we derive the exact mode-by-mode response of the mean Rademacher estimator and show that it selectively suppresses modes involving many parameters. For general generators, the filtered mean field need not be conservative. We derive its sampling variance, compare it with Gaussian random-direction smoothing, and formulate the resulting stochastic dynamics through a Fokker--Planck equation. The non-conservative field generates acceleration to the steady state and the global minimum of the cost function by gradually decreasing the parameters in SPSA.

The bound is sharp in dimension and holds for arbitrary test states, including those entangled across blocks. We give finite-alphabet approximation, conditioning and error bounds, and a matched-resource comparison with a Quantum Shannon reference compiler. The gate bound is exponential in the encoded qubit count and excludes classical preprocessing.

Abstract: Secret sharing is ubiquitous throughout cryptography. All possible access structures are classically feasible, and in the case of threshold access structures, the protocols are even very efficient. However, when moving to the quantum setting, the no-cloning theorem shows that many access structures are impossible. In fact, no-cloning exactly characterizes feasibility: for thresholds, quantum secret sharing is possible if and only if the threshold $t$ is strictly more than $n/2$.

In fact, because superluminal signalling might instead be avoided by relaxing other fundamental assumptions in the presence of superluminal reference frames, the proposed objective uncertainty may not be necessary at all. Our results clarify the limitations of uncertainty-based resolutions and the causal trade-offs faced by superluminal extensions of relativity.

We adapt their template to codes (spanned by the rows of $\mathbf{B}$) with a ``two-fold multiplication property'': the coordinate-wise products of pairs of codewords span a space of dimension much smaller than $m$.

For broad families of these constraints, we show that a uniformly random satisfying vector remains satisfying with constant probability after resampling a uniformly random coordinate. Abstract: We study quantum query complexity under several models of imperfect oracle access, and develop lower bounds through a common framework based on fractional block sensitivity (\(\fbs\)).
  For a negligent oracle that applies the correct query with probability \(1-p\), we prove a lower bound in terms of \(\fbs(f,0^n)\). We also show, perhaps surprisingly, that negligence need not destroy quantum speedups: any function \(f\) can be transformed into a partial function \(f'\) whose negligent query complexity essentially preserves the quantum query complexity of \(f\). This gives partial functions with exponential quantum speedups even under negligent queries, and in particular rules out a general lower bound in terms of \(\fbs(f)\) for partial functions in this model.

For hybrid algorithms using \(Q\) coherent and \(C\) classical queries, we prove the tradeoff \(C+Q^2=Ω(\fbs(f))\). For an IID dephasing noisy model where each query dephases the query-index register at rate \(p\) independently, we prove \(Ω\!\left(p\,\fbs(f)\right)\) queries are necessary.

Computing this resource reduces to a convex optimization problem, providing a simple way to derive lower bounds for new noise schedules. As applications, we recover the hybrid and IID dephasing bounds and determine the query complexity of unstructured search when the dephasing rate grows over time.

Abstract: We prove that quantum blind-unforgeability implies quantum plus-one unforgeability, settling an open question in the literature. Since plus-one unforgeability is known not to imply blind-unforgeability, our result establishes that blind-unforgeability is \emph{strictly stronger} than plus-one unforgeability in the quantum setting.

This enables us to transform any successful plus-one attacker into a blind-unforgeability attacker with only inverse-polynomial loss. Our proof represents a conceptual shift from merely extracting a useful query history as in earlier attempts, which we show inevitably fails in general, to first coherently reshaping how query histories interfere.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究在仅能访问非受控哈密顿量演化 $U_H$ 及其逆 $U_H^\dagger$ 的弱访问模型下，能否达到与使用受控哈密顿量演化相同量级的基态制备复杂度，并给出肯定回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基态制备算法依赖块编码或受控哈密顿量演化，已达到近最优复杂度。一个自然的问题是：受控哈密顿量演化是否必要？能否在仅提供非受控哈密顿量演化的更弱访问模型下达到同样的标度？

Result: 给定基态权重至少为 $\eta$、谱隙至少为 $\gamma$ 的输入态：第一算法需要 $\widetilde O(\alpha_H/(\gamma\eta))$ 次查询；第二算法改进为 $\widetilde O(\alpha_H/(\gamma\sqrt\eta))$。后者与已知下界相差对数因子，表明额外访问受控哈密顿量演化无法在超出对数因子的意义上改进基态制备的查询复杂度。框架进一步扩展到基态性质估计与能量估计，并给出二者的显式复杂度保证。

Both algorithms are based on a simple mechanism that we call the spectral descent principle, in which the algorithm starts from the input state $|ψ\rangle$ and progressively descends through the energy spectrum, moving closer to the ground state until the desired fidelity is reached. We also extend this framework to ground-state property and energy estimation and give explicit complexity guarantees for both tasks.

Abstract: Quantum simulations based on first-quantized approaches can offer lower space and gate complexities than second-quantized approaches. A more direct approach is to extract information about the Hamiltonian from measurements on the system itself. In many settings, however, direct access to the full system is unavailable and measurements can be performed only through controllable probes, as in quantum sensing and boundary spectroscopy. Existing Hamiltonian learning protocols under such restricted access typically require a special symmetry, a known parametric form, or controlled operations beyond free evolution. We introduce Quantum Krylov Learning (QKL), a restricted-access framework that avoids these requirements. QKL uses single temperature probe autocorrelators and their operator Krylov structure to extract model-independent spectral information about the unknown system. Rather than fitting a parametrized Hamiltonian, it reconstructs the operator Krylov Jacobi matrix representing the dynamical modes accessible to the probe. The method assumes that the unobserved system is at, or near, infinite temperature. Under this condition, the Jacobi matrix can be reconstructed directly from the autocorrelation of a probe observable, without oracle access to the Hamiltonian. The required operations are free evolution under the unknown $H$, together with probe state preparation and measurement. The learning core therefore requires no symmetry, no parametric model, and no controlled-unitary gates. We show a Lieb--Robinson bound for the operator Krylov chain and derive a data-driven criterion for the required observation window. We also establish an end-to-end sample complexity guarantee: under generic conditions on the spectral gaps and weights, the number of measurement shots required to achieve spectral precision $\varepsilon$ is polynomial in $1/\varepsilon$.

Abstract: Bell nonlocality allows secret key to be certified without trusting the internal operation of the devices, and even against eavesdroppers constrained only by the no-signaling principle. In our approach, Alice and Bob first establish operational equivalences among their local preparation and measurement procedures, which are then treated as theory-independent constraints that any physical intervention on the communication channel, including that of an adversary, must respect. Eve is otherwise unrestricted, that is, her intervention on the transmitted system is not assumed to be described by quantum theory. Within this adversarial model, we show that Eve's optimal guessing probability against individual attacks is the solution of a finite linear program, and hence that a worst-case asymptotic key rate follows from the observed statistics and the operational equivalences alone. Concretely, the (3,2)-parity-oblivious random access code, known to yield secret key against a quantum eavesdropper, yields none certifiable key rate against a post-quantum one. In this work, these alternative interpretations are confronted with data from time-resolved fluorescence spectroscopy experiments with erythrosine B and eosine Y as fluorophores. These results establish routing-aware pruning as a practical and unified compilation strategy across the quantum computing stack.

TL;DR: 本文检验了"负Kirkwood–Dirac准概率是相互作用无关测量与后选择计量量子优势来源"这一说法。结果表明：对单光子单次通过任意干涉仪，每吸收光子获得的透射率Fisher信息等于直接透射值减去虚部平方和，无干涉仪或读出方式能超越直接透射；负性仅在弱照明输出处集中信息，并不构成优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负Kirkwood–Dirac准概率长期被认为赋予了相互作用无关测量（interaction-free measurement）和后选择计量（postselected metrology）的量子优势。作者希望针对相互作用无关测量最初的设问——在让样品尽可能少吸收光的前提下获取样品信息——来检验这一论断是否成立。

Abstract: Remote state preparation is a fundamental quantum-information protocol that exploits prior knowledge of a target state to reduce the communication resources required for quantum-state distribution. Here, we propose a remote-state-preparation protocol for a stationary electron-spin qubit using hybrid entanglement between the electron spin and a coherent-state light pulse, generated by spin-dependent reflection from a spin-cavity system. Unlike single-photon encodings, the coherent-state encoding is naturally tolerant of photon loss: a loss channel attenuates the coherent-state amplitude, leading to a gradual reduction in preparation fidelity rather than probabilistic protocol failure. The central difficulty---implementing the projection onto superpositions of nonorthogonal coherent states required in the conventional protocol---is circumvented by coupling the transmitted pulse to an auxiliary spin, followed by homodyne detection and a spin projective measurement. The protocol therefore avoids direct coherent-state-superposition measurements and photon-number-resolving detection. In the ideal limit, deterministic preparation of a particular class of target states can be achieved using only one bit of classical communication. We analytically quantify the effects of coherent-state nonorthogonality, fiber loss, and spin dephasing on the preparation fidelity. Increasing the coherent-state amplitude improves state distinguishability and hence the preparation fidelity, but also enhances which-branch information leakage caused by photon loss, resulting in an optimal amplitude at each transmission distance. For experimentally relevant parameters, the optimized average fidelity remains well above the classical benchmark over distances of tens of kilometers. We also discuss a possible implementation using diamond nitrogen-vacancy centers.

Testing on a selection of tabular benchmark datasets, we find that NFS is an effective classifier architecture broadly competitive with established classical baselines, including a comparable Random Fourier Features model, and possessing comparable performance to data-reuploading QNNs; combined with additional analysis comparing the learned Fourier spectra of QNNs and NFS on synthetic data, these results establish NFS as a natural classical baseline for evaluating QNN performance.

Abstract: The equalities IP = PSPACE and QIP = PSPACE, the latter achievable with three messages, raise a basic question: how much of an interactive proof's power comes from the underlying physical theory? When they exchange only classical messages, protocols in every theory satisfying our standard assumptions decide exactly PSPACE. For protocols with a quantum verifier and quantum messages, allowing a prover to use any theory containing quantum theory does not increase the maximum acceptance probability. Thus, the three-message PSPACE result remains valid against such provers. When messages may be arbitrary systems, the interactive-proof class can strictly exceed PSPACE.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子纠错码中实现具有横向非Clifford操作的编码，是容错量子计算的重要目标。传统码空间搜索方法缺乏对横向对称性的系统刻画，且连续自由度来源不清晰。本文旨在通过构造不变母态的方式，系统性地生成具有指定横向对称性、且能支持非Clifford门集的码。

Abstract: Rare-earth (RE) atoms, such as erbium (Er), have inner-shell optical transitions in the near-infrared, some of which are in the telecom bands. These transitions are largely insensitive to the host environment due to effective shielding by the filled outer shells. The $4f \rightarrow 5d$ transition near 1299 nm forms a multiplet of narrow components, consistent with crystal-field splitting, and decays on a microsecond timescale. The $4f \rightarrow 4f$ transition near 1437 nm shows a matrix shift below 1 cm$^{-1}$ in both hosts. In solid Ne, its inhomogeneous linewidth is bounded by the spectrometer resolution to < 22 GHz, and its PL lifetime reaches up to 78.9 ms, close to the estimated radiative limit. Thermal annealing extends the 1437 nm lifetime in Ne and suppresses the phonon sidebands relative to the zero-phonon lines in Ar. These results identify RG solids as promising hosts for long-lived telecom-band Er emission and provide a foundation for future spin-photon interfaces and hybrid quantum architectures.

TATVA views circuit synthesis as a sequential decision problem in which the agents select one gate at a time and calculate the fidelity after each applied gate, with the gate producing the highest fidelity selected for the circuit. The system introduces a parallel architecture that uses two RL agents, Deep Q-Network (DQN) and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), along with Qiskit's statevector simulator. The system can synthesize up to 5 qubits while achieving a fidelity of 0.999999 and producing compact circuits. After achieving the desired fidelity, it performs a post-hoc optimization step that reduces the circuit depth while preserving the achieved fidelity. The circuit is finalized using fidelity, success rate, gate count, and circuit depth. Target states that are not used during training are used to test whether the system has learned effectively and can generalize beyond the training states. This approach aims to enable automatic quantum circuit synthesis with high fidelity using reinforcement learning.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明m个不相交CCZ门层在带干净辅助比特的Hadamard-free Clifford+T电路中恰好需要6m+1个T门，与Campbell和Howard的构造匹配，首次对一般m证明最优性（m=1时为经典值7，m=2时为13）。对任意受控酉门下界与精确计数相差不超过1，并恢复单控制m个Toffoli扇出的4m+3。在TODD优化器完成的24个基准电路上，该下界将其311个合并相位多项式块中的193个认证为在其Hadamard分层下最优（五个优化器种子下为186至193），优于稳定子零度的113个。

Conclusion: 所提出的迷向下界为对角级三门T门数提供了严格且可多项式时间计算的最优性证书，在基准电路上显著优于稳定子零度指标。该下界在仅含被酉性反计算临时AND块的内部Hadamard电路中也成立，但在自适应前馈情形下仅能证明t≥ν。

Abstract: The $T$-count is the dominant cost of fault-tolerant Clifford+$T$ computation. We prove that a layer of $m$ disjoint CCZ gates, the diagonal core of a parallel Toffoli layer, needs exactly $6m+1$ $T$ gates in every Hadamard-free Clifford+$T$ circuit with clean ancillas. Campbell and Howard gave the matching construction. To our knowledge this is the first proof that it is optimal for general $m$ (for $m=1$ the value $7$ is classical, and Campbell and Howard state the value $13$ at $m=2$). On every controlled unitary the same floor comes within one of their exact count, and it recovers their $4m+3$ for a fan-out of $m$ Toffolis from one control. The proof rests on an isotropy constraint: for a pure-cubic phase, the vectors recording which $T$ gates touch each qubit span a totally isotropic subspace. In general the constraint gives the isotropy floor $δ\ge2(n-d^{\ast})-r$ for every diagonal level-three gate, computed from the phase polynomial in polynomial time. The floor is never below stabilizer nullity $ν$, which equals $n-d^{\ast}$ on this class, and a separate parity argument raises it to $2ν+1$ on non-Clifford pure-cubic gates. On the output of the TODD optimizer for the $24$ benchmark circuits it completes, the floor certifies $193$ of its $311$ merged phase-polynomial blocks optimal for their Hadamard layering ($186$ to $193$ across five optimizer seeds), against $113$ for nullity. The floor also holds, under stated conditions, for circuits whose only internal Hadamards form unitarily uncomputed temporary AND blocks, while under adaptive feedforward only $t\geν$ is proved.

</details>

Abstract: Device-independent conference key agreement (DI-CKA) lets a group of parties establish a shared secret key from untrusted quantum devices, with security certified by non-locality. For coherent labels, we use an indexed path-recording framework to analyze interference across labels and prove security of a construction based on quantum-secure function and permutation primitives. Further applications include nonce-resolved quantum authentication, coherently masked QRAM queries, and efficient emulation of random multi-time dynamics. Our results extend quantum pseudorandomness from individual operations to efficiently generated families of random-looking quantum dynamics.

Result: 在去极化信道下对代表性QLDPC码的仿真表明，所提解码器在具有并行低时延结构的同时，提升了学习型顺序BP的可靠性。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes are promising candidates for low-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computation, but their practical use requires fast, low-complexity, and reliable decoders. Belief propagation (BP) is attractive because of its local message-passing structure, yet standard flooding BP often suffers from convergence failures on QLDPC codes due to short cycles, degeneracy, and symmetric decoding trajectories. Learned sequential BP improves convergence by using a reinforcement-learning policy to choose variable-node update orders, but a single learned trajectory can still be sensitive to unfavorable local Pauli decisions. We propose a learned parallel bit-flipping sequential BP decoder with a new quaternary score combining syndrome gain, a quantized Pauli log-likelihood penalty, and Q-table lookahead at hypothetical post-flip states. Our decoder first runs learned sequential BP for a fixed number of iterations. If the syndrome is not satisfied, it constructs quaternary bit-flipping candidates, each corresponding to changing the current Pauli decision of one qubit. The selected candidates initialize independent learned sequential BP continuations from the same decoder state. Since these continuations are independent, they can be executed in parallel, so testing several candidates mainly increases parallel hardware resources rather than sequential decoding latency. Simulations on representative QLDPC codes over the depolarizing channel show that the proposed decoder improves the reliability of learned sequential BP while having a parallel low-latency structure.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 平稳性检验为从热态学习哈密顿量提供了统一且高效的框架，将量子Gibbs采样工具与量子学习理论相连接，并在结构学习、亚稳态系数学习及面积律改进方面取得突破。

Abstract: The task of learning the Hamiltonian interactions governing a quantum system, given samples of its thermal (or 'Gibbs') states, is a foundational question at the intersection of quantum learning theory and many-body physics. In this paper, we draw connections to the quantum Gibbs sampling literature to introduce a natural learning algorithm we call the stationarity test: which simply "guesses" the Hamiltonian, and measures the rate-of-change of local observables, under the associated detailed-balanced quantum Markov chain [CKG23]. We leverage the stationarity test to address the following applications:
  1. To give the first learning algorithm for the underlying interaction graph, i.e. structure learning, of lattice Hamiltonians at all temperatures, given copies of their Gibbs states.
  2. To give the first learning algorithm for the coefficients of a lattice Hamiltonian, given only copies of its thermal metastable states, modeled as the "local minima" of the free energy.
  3. In addition, we present a refinement to the recent area law for thermal metastable states [BCV25], which holds in the thermodynamic limit.
  Our learning algorithms are rigorous, time-efficient, and nearly sample-optimal in system size and accuracy. At a technical level, our arguments are based on new approximate locality and convexity properties for the quantum Fisher information of these Gibbs sampling algorithms.

Abstract: We consider a cavity quantum electrodynamics (CQED) setup where an ensemble of spin-1 atoms driven by a classical field interacts with two driven cavity field modes. In order to visualize the limit cycle and synchronization in the field, we introduce generalized two-mode field Husimi-Q function using SU(2) coherent states. For atomic spin states, we make use of spin-1 Husimi-Q function to visualize the same. Additionally, we calculate Wehrl mutual information between the two modes and find that the field synchronization and Wehrl mutual information are mutually exclusive. Our study would be important to extract quantum advantages in quantum metrology using coupled atom-cavity systems.

Our modular designs greatly improve or resolve the per-oracle query complexity of open multi-oracle problems: (1) Hamiltonian simulation under $H=\sum_j H_j$ with weighted quasinorm cost $\approx t\langle\vecλ,\vec C\rangle_{1/2}+\mathcal{O}(\|\vec C\|_1\log(1/ε))$ for block-encoding normalizations $\vecλ$ and costs $\vec C$; (2) sparse simulation with optimal time--norm $Θ(\sqrt{d}\|H\|_{\rm 1\to2}t)$ and additive error dependence; (3) solving linear systems and (4) differential equations under minimal matrix assumptions with optimal weighted state-preparation and multi-term cost; (5) ground-state preparation and energy estimation; (6) generalized eigenvalue problems. Abstract: Measuring part of a many-body wave function generates a projected ensemble of pure quantum states on the unmeasured subsystem. Recent advances in deep thermalization have shown that, in chaotic systems, this ensemble universally converges to the maximally random ensemble compatible with its average density matrix, known as the Scrooge ensemble. By contrast, free-fermion systems are nonchaotic, and their quantum states are constrained to remain Gaussian. Motivated by this distinction, we introduce the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble to describe deep thermalization in generic free-fermion systems. This ensemble is defined as a distortion of the Gaussian Haar ensemble by the average density matrix, and its moments reveal an enlarged symmetry of Gaussian states in the replicated Hilbert space. We demonstrate the emergence of the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble in two complementary settings: (1) we analytically prove that the projected ensemble of the SYK$_2$ model follows the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble at arbitrary evolution times; (2) we provide numerical evidence that it emerges at sufficiently long times in random Gaussian circuits with charge conservation. Our results establish the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble as a universal description of deep thermalization in free-fermion systems.

</details>

Abstract: We present a direct experimental verification of quantum coherence for a set of quantum states in a basis-independent manner. Abstract: Measurement incompatibility is a fundamental quantum resource that enables advantages in many quantum information tasks, including cryptography and communication. However, unavoidable interactions between a system and its environment can degrade or even completely destroy measurement incompatibility; such a process is referred to as a measurement-incompatibility-annihilating channel. Recently, the capability of noisy quantum dynamics to preserve measurement incompatibility has been characterized within the resource theory of measurement incompatibility preservability. This motivates studying how to purify the preservation of measurement incompatibility and how to activate it from a measurement-incompatibility-annihilating channel. To this end, we first introduce our figures of merit as robustness-based resource monotones within this resource theory. We demonstrate that while pre-filtering operations can strengthen measurement incompatibility preservability, they cannot activate it from an incompatibility-annihilating channel, establishing a no-go theorem. Furthermore, we explicitly demonstrate that an incompatibility-annihilating channel can be stochastically activated via post-filtering operations. Our results provide a practical framework for exploiting measurement incompatibility in quantum information processing.

Abstract: Electric-field noise in microfabricated surface ion traps contributes to gate errors in trapped-ion quantum computers, but this noise has been challenging to predict. Abstract: Exploring the world of Bell inequalities requires numerical methods that scale effectively with the number of parties, inputs, and outputs. Such methods can be used to study large-scale Bell inequalities beyond the reach of analytic methods, to design experiments for violating more complicated Bell inequalities, and to enable applications of Bell inequality violation. Such applications include randomness generation and multi-agent coordination with restricted communication. However, current general purpose optimizers such as the see-saw method struggle to handle Bell inequalities with only a dozen inputs and outputs. We introduce an optimizer for the quantum value of a Bell inequality based on batched gradient descent (BGD). More precisely, our BGD optimizer is a differentiable search over feasible quantum strategies in which states and projective measurements are generated from unconstrained variables. The Bell expression is evaluated by a direct tensor contraction without forming the prohibitively large Bell operator. This formulation makes parallel random restarts inexpensive and is thus naturally implemented by a GPU. We evaluate our optimizer against the see-saw method and find a significant speedup for a wide variety of Bell inequality families. Implemented on a GPU, our BGD optimizer can optimize Bell inequalities in our numerical experiments with more than a thousand inputs or outputs within minutes. For these large-scale Bell inequalities, we also efficiently compute the classical value by enumerating possible strategies using a GPU or expressing the optimization as a mixed-integer linear program. Our optimizers can serve as numerical evaluation tools for Bell experiments and multi-agent coordination problems modeled by Bell inequalities with many inputs and outputs, a common feature of real-world scenarios such as high frequency trading and distributed systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局部测量反作用如何重塑相互作用量子多体系统中的探测统计，缺乏精确可解析的例子，因此需要构建一个可精确求解的模型来刻画局部测量与幺正动力学竞争的行为。

Result: 得到了首次探测概率的精确解析表达式，其统计呈现两种区域：测量频率较低时首次探测统计服从几何分布；测量频繁时，测量引发的关联导致统计偏离几何行为，偏离由测量探测时间与幺正电路弛豫时间尺度的竞争决定。返回概率受被监测子区域大小的控制。

Abstract: We study the interplay between local stroboscopic measurements and the unitary quantum circuit dynamics of the deterministic Floquet-quantum East model. We derive exact analytical results for the probability of first detecting a subset of qubits back in their initial state. This is achieved via a renewal equation linking the first-detection probability to the subsystem Loschmidt echo, which encodes the effective open dynamics of the monitored qubits. The resulting statistics exhibit two regimes set by the competition between the measurement probing time and the relaxation timescale of the unitary circuit dynamics: while infrequent monitoring leads to geometric first-detection statistics, frequent monitoring induces correlations that manifest as deviations from this geometric behavior. These results provide a rare exact characterization of how local measurement backaction reshapes detection statistics in an interacting quantum many-body system. In our local protocol, return probabilities are controlled by the size of the monitored subregion and can therefore be accessed on digital quantum simulators via local mid-circuit measurements.

</details>


### [84] [Less precise but less noisy: local circuits for momentum-space state preparation and measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01704)
*Etienne Granet,Henrik Dreyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Quantinuum H2量子计算机上，紧束缚链基态制备存在一个系统尺度N，超过该尺度后绝热演化在相同门数和电路深度下达到的能量显著低于费米傅里叶变换（FFT）；这种差异源于FFT的高动量分辨率需要实空间长程耦合，从而加速误差传播。作者提出一种精度较低但成本更低、噪声更小的动量测量方案，在谱函数测量中表现优于FFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法通常以门数或电路深度为优化目标，但本文认为噪声敏感性是同样重要却常被忽视的指标。作者试图揭示：为追求高精度（高动量分辨率）而设计的长程耦合电路会更快传播误差，因此在含噪硬件上未必优于局域物理电路。

Method: 在Quantinuum System Model H2量子计算机上，对紧束缚链基态制备任务，在相同门数与相同电路深度条件下比较绝热演化与费米傅里叶变换（FFT）的表现，并分析两者对噪声的敏感性差异。此外提出一种新的动量测量方案，并将其用于谱函数测量，与FFT进行对比。

Result: 实验发现在某一系统尺度N之外，绝热演化在相同门数和相同电路深度下达到的能量显著低于FFT。作者将FFT的高噪声敏感性归因于其高精度：能区分1/N量级的动量，而这需要实空间长程耦合，进而加速误差传播；局域物理电路（如绝热演化）动量分辨率较粗，但误差传播更慢。所提动量测量方案精度虽低于FFT，但成本更低、噪声更小，在H2上谱函数测量性能优于FFT。

Conclusion: 对于实际物理应用，高动量分辨率很少是必需的，往往值得用低噪声敏感性来换取。研究强调，在门数和电路深度之外，降低量子算法的噪声敏感性同样至关重要。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are usually optimized for gate count or circuit depth. We find on Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer that for a tight-binding chain ground state preparation, there is a system size $N$ beyond which the adiabatic evolution reaches significantly lower energies than the Fermionic Fourier Transform (FFT), with the same number of gates, and with the same circuit depth. We attribute this high noise sensitivity of the FFT to its high precision, being able to distinguish momenta by $1/N$. This high resolution in momentum space requires long-range couplings in real space, which propagates errors faster. In contrast, although local and physical circuits such as the adiabatic evolution have a coarser momentum resolution, they also propagate errors more slowly. For physical applications, high momentum resolution is rarely required and is often worth trading for low noise sensitivity. We also introduce a momentum measurement scheme that although less precise than FFT, is less costly and less noisy. We show that it achieves better performance than FFT for spectral function measurement on Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer. Our work emphasizes the importance of reducing the noise sensitivity of quantum algorithms, beyond the number of gates or circuit depth.

</details>


### [85] [Near-optimal quantum query lower bounds on bipartiteness and expansion testing in the bounded-degree graph model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01752)
*Chandrima Kayal,Sayantan Sen,Dániel Szabó*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在受限度数模型下研究图属性测试中二分性与扩展性测试的量子查询复杂度，证明了这两个问题本质上紧的 Ω̃(N^{1/3}) 量子查询下界，从而将其量子查询复杂度刻画到多对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情形下二分性与扩展性测试均需 Θ̃(√N) 查询；此前 Ambainis 等给出了 O(N^{1/3}) 的量子算法并证明扩展性测试需 Ω̃(N^{1/4})，但二分性测试能否有指数级量子优势一直悬而未决，且十余年无进展，因此需要确定二者真正的量子查询复杂度。

Method: 采用多项式方法，但不同于直接分析主问题，构造与主问题通过归约相关联的中间问题，并对所得多项式进行更精细的分析，从而导出近似最优的下界。

Result: 证明了二分性测试与扩展性测试均具有 Ω̃(N^{1/3}) 的量子查询下界，与已知的 O(N^{1/3}) 上界在多对数因子内匹配，完全刻画了这两个问题的量子查询复杂度。

Conclusion: 二分性与扩展性测试在受限度数模型下的量子查询复杂度为 Θ̃(N^{1/3})，消除了二分性测试可能存在指数级量子优势的可能性，并解决了该领域悬置十余年的开放问题。

Abstract: In this work, we study bipartiteness and expansion testing, two canonical problems in graph property testing in the bounded-degree model through the lens of quantum query complexity. In the classical setting, it is known that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{N})$ queries are necessary and sufficient for both these testing problems (Goldreich and Ron, 1999, 2000 & 2002), where $N$ denotes the number of vertices of the input graph. Due to their significance, (Ambainis, Childs, and Liu, 2011) initiated the study of these problems in the quantum setting and designed quantum algorithms for bipartiteness and expansion testing that perform $\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$ queries, showing a polynomial speedup. They also proved that $\widetildeΩ(N^{1/4})$ queries are necessary for expansion testing, but the possibility of an exponential quantum advantage for bipartiteness testing remained open. Despite significant effort, there has been no improvement in these results in the last decade and a half. In this work, we prove essentially tight $\widetildeΩ(N^{1/3})$ quantum query lower bounds for both bipartiteness and expansion testing, thereby completely characterizing the quantum query complexity of these problems up to polylogarithmic factors. While our proofs use the polynomial method similarly to Ambainis, Childs, and Liu, we use intermediate problems that we relate to the main problems via reductions, and perform a more precise analysis of the resulting polynomials, leading to the near-optimal lower bounds.

</details>


### [86] [Efficient quantum phase estimation with adaptive entanglement-assisted Hadamard test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01772)
*Hengzhun Chen,Benchi Zhao,Yingzhou Li*

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应纠缠辅助Hadamard检验（AEHT），通过迭代精化参考相位实现渐进增强的相位放大，在设备重启计数度量下优于传统方法，可用于近-term量子处理器上的高精度量子相位估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠辅助Hadamard检验（EHT）虽有放大相位信号的能力，但放大倍数受参考相位精度限制，在高精度区间效率低下；此外在不完美本征态制备情形下，传统EHT存在系统性偏差。

Method: 提出自适应纠缠辅助Hadamard检验（AEHT），迭代精化参考相位，使放大能力随迭代逐步增强；同时引入设备重启计数（device-restart count）替代shot count作为相位估计代价度量，并考虑不完美本征态制备场景。

Conclusion: 通过释放大规模纠缠态的完整放大能力，本工作为近-term量子处理器上高效估计高精度量子相位提供了可行方法。

Abstract: The entanglement-assisted Hadamard test (EHT) is a practical method for estimating a quantum phase by amplifying the phase signal. However, the feasible amplification is fundamentally limited by the accuracy of the reference phase, such that the method is inefficient in the high-precision regime. In this work, we propose an algorithm, called adaptive entanglement-assisted Hadamard test (AEHT), that iteratively refines the reference phase, enabling progressively stronger amplification as the iteration goes by. We further consider the imperfect eigenstate preparation scenario, where a systematic bias is unavoidable when estimating the quantum phase with the conventional EHT. Such a bias can be suppressed by the proposed AEHT. Moreover, taking physical implementation into consideration, we adopt device-restart count to measure the cost of quantum phase estimation, rather than shot count. The numerical experiments confirm the effectiveness of the proposed AEHT compared with conventional methods under this measure. By unlocking the full amplification power of large entangled states, this work offers an efficient method to estimate high-precision quantum phase on near-term quantum processors.

</details>


### [87] [QUFIG: GNN-Based Prediction of Quantum Fault Injection Vulnerabilities with Gate-Level Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01777)
*Shihan Zhao,Qiying Li,Ben Dong,Qian Wang,Yuntao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QUFIG，将门级故障分析建模为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题，用基于电路DAG的GNN预测每个门-故障对的脆弱性分数，实验表明可在减少2.9%–19.8%需检查门数的同时有效识别故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件的规模与可访问性增长，使云平台出现运行时故障注入攻击等可靠性与安全问题。现有工作无法以门级精度识别脆弱点，也难以适应运行时环境。

Method: 将门级故障分析形式化为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题；采用基于电路DAG的GNN骨干网络，预测每个门对每种注入故障类型的脆弱性分数，该分数定义为门-故障对对电路保真度的影响；随后按脆弱性分数对门-故障对排序。

Result: 在QASMbench与HamLib MaxCut上的实验中，QUFIG相比随机和基于深度的启发式方法，能以更少检查次数恢复高影响的脆弱门-故障对，并将需检查的门数减少2.9%–19.8%，同时保持有效的故障识别。

Conclusion: QUFIG支持量子电路设计者更高效地识别脆弱性并实施针对性防御。

Abstract: The growing scale and accessibility of quantum hardware exposed new reliability and security challenges in the quantum computing workflow, such as the run-time fault injection attacks in cloud-based quantum computing platforms. However, existing works fail to identify vulnerabilities with gate-level precision or adapt to run-time environments. In this work, we formulate gate-level fault analysis as a learning-guided prioritization problem under restricted fidelity budgets. The framework uses a circuit-DAG-based GNN backbone to predict the vulnerability score of each gate to each type of injected fault, defined as the impact of the gate-fault pair on circuit fidelity. The gate-fault pairs are then ranked by their vulnerability score. Experiments on QASMbench and HamLib MaxCut show that QUFIG recovers high-impact vulnerable gate-fault pairs with fewer inspections than random and depth-based heuristics. Our results show that QUFIG can reduce the number of gates requiring inspection by 2.9--19.8% while maintaining effective fault identification, allowing quantum circuit designers to identify vulnerabilities and apply targeted defenses more efficiently.

</details>


### [88] [Non-Markovian effects on informational steady states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01782)
*Jacob Werner*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息稳态(ISSs)出现在连续测量获得信息的速率与早期测量信息流失到环境的速率恰好平衡时。虽然ISSs已在马尔可夫设定下被研究，但记忆效应如何影响其形成及稳态性质仍知之甚少。

Method: 在连续监测碰撞模型框架下研究非马尔可夫效应对ISSs的影响；将环境建模为一系列双量子比特辅助单元(ancillas)。

Result: 对于该特定模型，稳态每次测量的信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关；数值结果进一步刻画了系统-辅助单元相互作用参数与辅助单元-辅助单元相互作用参数如何影响非马尔可夫度和稳态信息增益。

Conclusion: 在该连续监测碰撞模型中，非马尔可夫性(记忆效应)会削弱稳态信息增益，且其强弱可由系统-辅助单元及辅助单元间的相互作用参数调控。

Abstract: Informational steady states (ISSs) arise when the rate at which information is gained from continuous measurements is exactly balanced by the rate at which information from earlier measurements is lost to the environment. While ISSs have been studied in Markovian settings, relatively little is known about how memory effects influence their formation and steady-state properties. In this work, we investigate non-Markovian effects on ISSs within the framework of continuously monitored collision models. We model the environment as a sequence of two-qubit ancillas and find that, for this specific model, the steady-state information gain per measurement is negatively correlated with the degree of non-Markovianity. Numerical results further characterize how the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction parameters influence both the degree of non-Markovianity and the steady-state information gain.

</details>


### [89] [Learnt Attacks on Quantum Key Distribution under Channel Noise and Device Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01792)
*Marcel Mordarski,Benjamin Gras,Abdelrahman Shehata,Daniel Budina,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将自适应窃听建模为受约束马尔可夫决策过程,在漂移信道下联合搜索门结构与旋转角度以学习攻击策略,量化了自适应相对最优固定电路的增益；在E91和BB84上分别达到上界的98%和99%,并复现了解析窃听方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QKD安全性分析基于平稳信道假设,而实际信道在两次校准之间会漂移。此前工作(如Decker等)仅在固定门模板上训练参数化电路对抗固定信道,无法回答"无法改变信道自身噪声、但可跟随漂移的窃听者能获得多少增益"这一未量化问题。

Method: 1) 将自适应窃听形式化为受约束马尔可夫决策过程:攻击者每轮选择一个电路,噪声服从Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程,约束为每个轮次块上的预算(中止条件)。2) 用最优固定电路给出下界,用动态规划给出上界。3) 提出学习型攻击:联合搜索门结构与旋转角度,得到足够紧凑的离散动作集,可推广到无已知模板的噪声模型(含振幅阻尼信道)。

Result: 在双边去极化噪声下的设备无关E91中,强化学习攻击者将Holevo信息从最优固定电路的0.135提升至零误检时的0.348,达到上界的98%。在漂移比特翻转信道下的BB84中,其保真度超出保守的噪声索引规则0.024,达到上界的99%。平稳噪声下,攻击者从基不对称性获得的增益在"平均误码率约束"与"逐基误码率约束"之间改变符号。从随机门序列出发的搜索能恢复解析克隆器和集体攻击密钥率,并从上方满足Winick--Lütkenhaus--Coles目标的下界。

Conclusion: 自适应窃听相对固定策略具有显著且有上界约束的增益;学习型联合结构-角度搜索是量化此类增益的有效工具,可处理缺乏已知模板的噪声信道,并能复现已知最优攻击,为漂移信道下QKD的实际安全裕度评估提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) links are provisioned from security analyses of stationary channels, whereas the devices that determine the channel drift between recalibrations. Whether an eavesdropper who cannot alter the channel's own noise gains by following that drift has not been quantified. Adaptive eavesdropping is posed here as a constrained Markov decision process in which the attacker selects one circuit per round while the noise level follows an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process and the abort condition is a budget over each block of rounds. The value of adaptation is bounded by the best fixed circuit and a dynamic-programming upper bound. The actions are learnt attacks. Whereas Decker et al. trained a parametrised circuit on a fixed gate template against a fixed channel, here the gate structure and rotation angles are searched jointly. This yields circuits compact enough to form a discrete action set, extending the construction to noise models lacking a known template, including the amplitude damping channel. On device-independent E91 under bilateral depolarising noise, a reinforcement-learning attacker raises her Holevo information from $0.135$ for the best fixed circuit to $0.348$ at zero detection, $98\%$ of the upper bound. On BB84 under a drifting bit-flip channel, she exceeds a conservative noise-indexed rule by $0.024$ in fidelity, reaching $99\%$ of the upper bound. Under stationary noise, the attacker's gain from basis asymmetry changes sign between an averaged and a per-basis error-rate constraint. The search, started from random gate sequences, recovers the analytical cloners and the collective-attack key rate, and meets the lower bound of the Winick--Lütkenhaus--Coles objective from above.

</details>


### [90] [Quantum Optimal Transport Barycenters: Existence, Duality, and Gaussian Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01855)
*Augusto Gerolin,Zhiyi Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立量子最优传输（QOT）重心框架，在可分Hilbert空间上对一般（可能无界）传输代价给出存在性与对偶结果，并统一处理2-量子Wasserstein重心。核心是高斯刚性：若至少一个高斯输入是faithful，则重心在所有量子态中唯一且必为高斯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Wasserstein重心的理论推广到量子态，为2-量子Wasserstein距离（Caglioti–Golse–Mouhot–Paul与De Palma–Trevisan的量子态/量子通道版本）提供统一处理；并回答高斯刚性这一关键问题——高斯输入是否迫使2-QW重心仍为高斯且在全体量子态中唯一。

Method: 1）构建量子最优传输重心框架，在可分Hilbert空间上对广泛（可能无界）传输代价证明存在性与对偶性；2）通过取与2-QW距离对应的典范二次代价算子，统一量子态与量子通道两种表述；3）证明重心问题存在高斯极小元，并约化为协方差矩阵上的有限维凸优化；4）针对"最优协方差唯一不蕴含量子态唯一"的困难，引入协方差互补松弛下的状态重构原理，将最优协方差的唯一性提升为整个量子态的唯一性。

Result: 重心问题存在高斯极小元并等价于协方差矩阵上的有限维凸优化；通过协方差互补松弛的状态重构原理，证明全局刚性定理：只要至少一个高斯输入是faithful，则重心在全体量子态中唯一且必为高斯。faithful为充分非必要条件：纯态族仍可确定唯一重心，而部分纯的非faithful高斯输入可能允许多个重心。

Conclusion: 该框架给出了2-量子Wasserstein重心的统一理论及高斯刚性刻画，明确了faithful条件在唯一性中的作用：充分但非必要；并揭示了非faithful情形下可能出现的多重心现象。

Abstract: We develop a Quantum Optimal Transport (QOT) barycenter framework for quantum states, as an analog of Wasserstein barycenters~\cite{AguCar}, and establish existence and duality results for a broad class of possibly unbounded transport costs on separable Hilbert spaces. Our framework provides, in particular, a unified treatment of $2$-quantum Wasserstein (QW) barycenters in both the quantum-state and quantum-channel formulations by specializing to the canonical quadratic cost operators associated with the $2$-quantum Wasserstein distances of Caglioti--Golse--Mouhot--Paul~\cite{caglioti2021towards,Golse-Mouhot-Paul:2016} and De Palma--Trevisan~\cite{DPaTre19}.
  The central results concern Gaussian input states and, in particular, Gaussian rigidity: whether Gaussian input states force the 2-QW barycenter itself to be Gaussian and uniquely determined when the minimization is taken over all quantum states. We first show that the barycenter problem admits a Gaussian minimizer and reduces to a finite-dimensional convex optimization problem over covariance matrices.
  The main difficulty is that uniqueness of the optimal covariance does not, in general, imply uniqueness of the underlying quantum state. We bridge this gap through a state-reconstruction principle under covariance complementary slackness that upgrades uniqueness of the optimal covariance to uniqueness of the full quantum state, and thereby prove a global rigidity theorem: if at least one Gaussian input is faithful, then the barycenter is unique among all quantum states and is necessarily Gaussian. Faithfulness is sufficient but not necessary: families of pure inputs still determine a unique barycenter, whereas partially pure nonfaithful Gaussian inputs may admit multiple barycenters.

</details>


### [91] [(A Variant of) Clifford Circuit Synthesis is NP-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02029)
*Luna Lima Keller,Richard Kueng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最优Clifford电路合成问题，证明了其某个变体是NP-hard的，从而与已知的3-SAT启发式方法形成复杂度上的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优电路合成（在给定基本门集下寻找最短深度电路）是量子与经典硬件设计的核心问题。经典版本的复杂度已有充分理解（启发式与严格硬度结果兼备），但量子版本所知甚少，尤其是最优Clifford电路合成的复杂度下界尚不清晰。

Method: 证明分两部分：(i) 将3-正则图上的3-边着色问题归约到仅涉及CZ门的电路合成问题；(ii) 证明额外可用的基本Clifford门（最主要的是Hadamard、phase和CNOT）不会带来最优电路深度的进一步改进。

Result: 最优Clifford合成的一个变体是NP-hard的。该结果与已有的基于3-SAT的启发式方法相匹配，精确化了Clifford电路的复杂度图景：仿真与等价性检查属于P，而判定一个Clifford酉算子能否在给定深度内实现则是NP-完全的。

Conclusion: 本文给出了最优Clifford电路合成的匹配硬度结果，明确了Clifford电路在仿真/等价性检查（P）与深度受限可实现性判定（NP-完全）之间的复杂度鸿沟。

Abstract: Optimal circuit synthesis is the problem of finding the shortest-depth circuit representation of a given functionality with respect to a pre-specified elementary gate set. This is a central problem in both quantum and classical hardware design. While the classical version is very well understood -- both in terms of heuristics and rigorous hardness assertions -- much less is known about optimal quantum circuit synthesis.
  We focus on optimal Clifford circuit synthesis, for which various heuristics are known, e.g. via reduction to 3-SAT. Our main result supplies a matching hardness result: a variant of optimal Clifford synthesis is NP-hard. The proof proceeds in two parts: (i) reduce 3-edge colorability on 3-regular graphs to a circuit synthesis problem that only involves CZ gates, (ii) prove that the availability of additional elementary Clifford gates -- most notably: Hadamard, phase and CNOT -- cannot lead to further improvements of the optimal circuit depth. Our work sharpens the complexity-theoretic understanding of Clifford circuits: simulation and equivalence checking are in P, whereas deciding whether a Clifford unitary admits an implementation within a prescribed depth is NP-complete.

</details>


### [92] [Sparse Hamiltonian simulation with optimal dependence on the maximum column Euclidean norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02030)
*Zecheng Li,Chunhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模拟 d-稀疏厄米哈密顿量的量子算法，查询复杂度为 O(tΛ√d + √d·log(2/ε))，去除了 Low 算法中的次多项式开销，并达到最坏情况下的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low [STOC 2019] 的 d-稀疏哈密顿量模拟算法存在次多项式开销，精度依赖并非最优，且在稀疏量子线性系统求解与黑箱酉算符实现等应用上未达到理论下界，因此需要一种更紧、更高效的量子模拟方法。

Method: 针对 d-稀疏厄米哈密顿量 H，假设已知最大列欧几里得范数 ‖H‖_{1→2} 的上界 Λ。当 tΛ≥1/2 时，通过稀疏查询预言机实现算子范数误差 ε 的模拟，查询次数为 O(tΛ√d + √d·log(2/ε))；其中精度项以加法对数形式出现，替代了 Low 算法中的次多项式开销。方法可改用已知的谱范数上界替代 Λ，1- 和 2-量子比特门数与查询规模线性相关。

Result: 对于 d>1 且 tΛ≥log(2/ε)，该查询上界与最坏情况下界匹配。应用中，求解满足 ‖A‖≤1、‖A^{-1}‖≤κ 的 d-稀疏量子线性系统需 O(κ√d·polylog(κ/ε)) 次查询；对每行每列至多 d 个非零元的黑箱酉算符，在稀疏访问及其伴随下需 O(√d·log(2/ε)) 次查询。常数误差下查询数最优，对任意 N×N 酉算符为 Θ(√N)，解决了 Berry 和 Childs [QIC 2012] 提出的黑箱酉实现开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了接近最优的 d-稀疏哈密顿量量子模拟算法，显著改进了查询复杂度并达到下界；同时为稀疏量子线性系统求解和黑箱酉算符实现提供了门高效的方案，在常数误差下达到最优查询复杂度 Θ(√N)，解决了长期存在的开放问题。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for simulating a $d$-sparse Hermitian Hamiltonian $H$, assuming a known upper bound $Λ$ on its maximum column Euclidean norm $\|H\|_{1\to2}$. For $tΛ\ge1/2$, simulation with operator-norm error $ε$ uses \[
  O\!\left(tΛ\sqrt d+\sqrt d\log(2/ε)\right) \] sparse-oracle queries. This removes the subpolynomial overhead in Low's algorithm [STOC 2019], replacing it with an additive logarithmic precision term. For $d>1$ and $tΛ\ge\log(2/ε)$, the bound matches the worst-case lower bound. A known spectral-norm upper bound may also be used in place of $Λ$. The number of 1- and 2-qubit gates is linear in the query scale, up to oracle costs and polynomial overhead in the input bit lengths and logarithmic precision parameters.
  As applications, we obtain $O(κ\sqrt d\,\mathrm{polylog}(κ/ε))$ queries for solving $d$-sparse quantum linear systems with $\|A\|\le1$ and $\|A^{-1}\|\leκ$, under standard sparse and state-preparation access. We also give a gate-efficient implementation of black-box unitaries with at most $d$ nonzero entries per row and column using $O(\sqrt d\log(2/ε))$ queries, given sparse access to the unitary and its adjoint. At constant error, the query bound is optimal and yields $Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries for arbitrary $N\times N$ unitaries, resolving the open question on black-box unitary implementation posed by Berry and Childs [QIC 2012].

</details>


### [93] [Adaptivity is all you need: Optimal stabilizer learning using just single-copy measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02031)
*L. Bittel,J. Eisert,W. Gong,A. A. Mele,L. Schatzki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明自适应单拷贝Clifford测量可用Θ(n)个样本学习n量子比特稳定子态，弥合了此前自适应与Bell测量之间的样本复杂度差距，并将结果推广到稳定子零度受限的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子态是量子纠错、基准测试与经典模拟的核心，但其可学习性存在显著差距：两拷贝Bell测量仅需Θ(n)个样本，而非自适应单拷贝测量需Ω(n²)个。作者旨在探究自适应能否弥合这一差距，并拓展至非精确稳定子态。

Method: 提出一种多项式时间自适应算法，仅使用单拷贝Clifford测量学习任意n量子比特稳定子态；基于相同思想构造样本最优的单拷贝容错检验器；在k个量子比特量子内存下给出最优检验权衡；并将自适应机制推广至稳定子零度至多为r的态（含T门数量受限的Clifford电路制备态）。

Result: 自适应算法以Θ(n)个单拷贝Clifford测量学习稳定子态，达到Bell采样的最优样本复杂度且无需多拷贝测量；得到样本最优的单拷贝容错检验器；在k个量子比特量子内存下实现最优检验权衡Θ(n−k+1/ε)（失真度ε）；稳定子零度≤r的态可用O(n2^r)个单拷贝测量学习。

Conclusion: 自适应性可完全弥合稳定子态学习在单拷贝与Bell测量间的样本复杂度差距，且该机制可推广至稳定子零度受限的态，为单拷贝量子学习与检验提供样本最优方案。

Abstract: Stabilizer states are central to quantum computing, underlying quantum error correction, benchmarking, and efficient classical simulation. Yet their learnability exhibits a striking gap: an $n$-qubit stabilizer state can be learned from $Θ(n)$ copies using two-copy Bell measurements, whereas non-adaptive single-copy measurements require $Ω(n^2)$ copies. Here we show that adaptivity completely closes this gap. We give a polynomial-time adaptive algorithm that learns an arbitrary $n$-qubit stabilizer state from $Θ(n)$ single-copy Clifford measurements, matching the optimal sample complexity of Bell sampling without any multi-copy measurements. The same ideas yield a sample-optimal single-copy tolerant tester and, with $k$ qubits of quantum memory, the optimal testing tradeoff $Θ(n-k+1/\varepsilon)$ at infidelity $\varepsilon$. Finally, we show that this adaptive mechanism extends beyond exact stabilizer states: states of stabilizer nullity at most $r$, including states prepared by Clifford circuits with a bounded number of $T$ gates, can be learned using $O(n2^{r})$ single-copy measurements.

</details>


### [94] [Nonlocality without entanglement for multipartite quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02032)
*Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明"两量子比特乘积测量"在联合访问下可完美区分，但在LOCC（局域量子操作与经典通信）下不可，从而给出"无纠缠非局域性"的测量版本，并揭示LOCC区分策略的层级、方向性及多体推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的"无纠缠非局域性"主要针对量子态，而测量的类比虽被视为可能但对简单设置被广泛否定；作者希望证明测量版本的非局域性其实可在极为简单的两量子比特乘积测量下出现，并系统刻画其LOCC区分结构。

Method: 构造两量子比特乘积测量对，分析其联合（全局）可区分性与LOCC不可区分性；比较自适应与非自适应LOCC协议（用六组双体qutrit乘积测量实现分离）；考察由哪一方发起协议造成的方向性；推广到n量子比特乘积测量对，使其全局可完美区分但在任意二分下、即便允许单侧任意联合量子操作也不可完美区分；并用基本光学元件实现基于量子过程非局域性的数据隐藏。

Result: 给出两量子比特乘积测量的全局可完美区分但LOCC不可区分的实例；展示自适应LOCC严格优于受限非自适应LOCC（六组双体qutrit乘积测量）；证明LOCC可区分性可本质地具有方向性；构造n量子比特乘积测量对在任意二分下均不可完美区分；实现基于量子过程无纠缠非局域性的量子数据隐藏的光学方案。

Conclusion: 测量也存在"无纠缠非局域性"，且比态版本更简单；LOCC区分存在自适应/非自适应层级与方向性；该现象可推广至多体及任意二分，并可用于光学数据隐藏。

Abstract: We show that a pair of two-qubit product measurements can be perfectly distinguished when accessed jointly, yet cannot be perfectly distinguished when their local components are distributed between distant parties restricted to local quantum operations and classical communication (LOCC). This establishes a measurement analogue of nonlocality without entanglement that, unlike its state-based counterpart, emerges in a remarkably simple setting. We further uncover a hierarchy of LOCC discrimination strategies: adaptive protocols, in which the probe used at one site may depend on classical information obtained from another, can strictly outperform restricted non-adaptive protocols, as demonstrated by a set of six bipartite qutrit product measurements. We also show that LOCC distinguishability of bipartite measurements can be intrinsically directional, depending on which party initiates the protocol. More generally, we construct pairs of $n$-qubit product measurements that are globally perfectly distinguishable but cannot be perfectly distinguished across any bipartition, even when all parties on either side of the bipartition are allowed arbitrary joint quantum operations. Finally, we show how quantum data hiding based on quantum-process nonlocality without entanglement can be realized using elementary optical components.

</details>


### [95] [Complexity and Applications of Nearest Stabilizer Product State Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02037)
*Daniel Grier,Hakop Pashayan,Luke Schaeffer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稳定子乘积态最近邻问题：给定n量子比特稳定子态|ψ⟩和单量子比特稳定子态集合S，最大化|⟨ψ|φ_1,…,φ_n⟩|²，并给出完整复杂度分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子乘积态优化问题在经典模拟算法运行时界、纠缠度量和低秩矩阵补全复杂度等多个场景中有应用价值，因此需要系统研究其可解性与计算复杂度。

Method: 通过改变单量子比特稳定子态集合S，研究该最大化问题的求解；在考虑Clifford群对称性后，对可能的集合S进行分类，并分析每类问题的计算复杂度。

Result: 在Clifford群对称性下，S共有9种本质不同的可能集合；除最简单的两类外，其余问题均为NP完全。

Conclusion: 该最近稳定子乘积态问题获得了完整的复杂度分类，且其解可用于经典模拟运行时界、纠缠度量和低秩矩阵补全等场景。

Abstract: Consider the following optimization problem over stabilizer product states: given an $n$-qubit stabilizer state $\left|ψ\right\rangle$ and a set of single-qubit stabilizer states $S$, maximize $\left|\langle ψ| φ_1, \ldots, φ_n \rangle\right|^2$ over single-qubit stabilizer states $\left|φ_i\right\rangle \in S$. By varying the set $S$, we show that solutions to this problem can be useful in a variety of settings: tighter runtime bounds for certain classical simulation algorithms; measures of entanglement; and the complexity of low-rank matrix completion. Moreover, we give a complete complexity classification of this nearest stabilizer product state problem. After accounting for the symmetries in the Clifford group, there are $9$ distinct possible sets $S$, and we show that all but the two simplest of these are $\NP$-complete.

</details>


### [96] [Fermionic Gaussianity can be tested with mode-independent sample-complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02050)
*Maxwell West,Martin Larocca*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种用于上界"远离目标族状态"的接受概率的新技术，从而上界判定问题的样本复杂度；应用于费米高斯态判定，证明Bell采样以最优的Θ(ε^{-2})副本数完成判定，且对任意集体/自适应测量仍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定未知量子态是否属于某状态族、或与整个族相距较远，是量子信息论的自然问题。通常存在自然的2副本测试，当态属于目标族时必然接受，但对远离族的态，其上界接受概率（从而样本复杂度）很难分析。论文旨在为该类判定问题提供通用且简单的最优性分析工具。

Method: 提出一种简单的通用技术，用于对一般状态族上界"远离族的态"在2副本测试中的接受概率，进而上界判定问题本身的样本复杂度。以费米高斯态为范例：分析Bell采样在该判定任务中的表现，并与任意集体测量和自适应测量协议的下界进行比较；另将方法推广至Slater行列式态族的类似判定问题。

Result: 证明判定未知纯n模态是否为费米高斯态、或与所有高斯态在迹距离上至少ε远，可用最优副本数Θ(ε^{-2})完成；该分析适用于已知的Bell采样过程，因此证明其对费米高斯性检验是最优的，即使相比允许任意集体与自适应测量的协议亦然。对Slater行列式态族，该类似判定问题同样可在与模数无关的样本复杂度下求解。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用上界技术可有效刻画2副本测试对远离目标族态的接受概率，进而给出判定问题的最优样本复杂度。Bell采样对费米高斯性判定达到最优，且对Slater行列式态族的判定亦具模数无关的样本复杂度，表明该框架具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Deciding whether an unknown quantum state either belongs to, or is far from, a given family of states is a natural question of quantum information theory. In many such cases, there is a natural 2-copy test which always accepts whenever the state indeed belongs to the target family; it is often much more difficult, however, to bound the probability of acceptance for states that are far from the family. Here we develop a simple new technique for upper bounding this probability for general families, and therefore upper bounding the sample-complexity of the decision problem itself. As a particularly striking example of this framework, we show that deciding whether an unknown pure $n$-mode state is fermionic Gaussian, or at least $\varepsilon$-far in trace distance from all Gaussian states, can be accomplished using the optimal number $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ of copies. Remarkably, our analysis applies to the well-known Bell sampling procedure for testing fermionic Gaussianity, which we therefore show to be optimal, even compared to protocols making arbitrary collective and adaptive measurements. As a second example, we show that the analogous decision problem for the family of Slater determinant states can also be solved with mode-independent sample-complexity.

</details>


### [97] [Sequential Capacity of Quantum Processes with Finite Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02068)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"序贯响应容量"（sequential response capacity）这一指标，用以量化固定内部记忆的量子器件在长时间运行下响应复杂度的增长规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是回答一个核心问题：在内部记忆大小固定的前提下，量子器件随着运行时间延长，其响应能力（可区分的过程数量）能复杂到何种程度。作者希望厘清量子与经典系统在相同资源约束下的能力差异，并推导容量与运行长度、概率分辨率之间的紧致定量关系。

Method: 将复杂度定义为序贯响应容量：即在每一测试阶段使用全新一次运行、以给定的响应概率间隔区分不同过程时，可持续进行的自适应测试阶段数。通过构造性协议（在单个可见量子比特上施加时间依赖的相位旋转、不增加内部记忆），并推导上下界来分析容量随运行步数 K 的增长；同时将结果与固定基下测量的经典随机过程进行对比，并引入由经典标签选择的相位序列，量化已知独立泡利噪声的影响。

Result: 在固定系统与记忆尺寸下，建立了容量与运行长度、概率分辨率之间的紧致规律：固定分辨率时，容量按 K log K 量级增长，且所构造协议达到该增长率，其测试给出的响应概率恰为 0 或 1；相同测试下，每步固定基测量的经典随机过程在固定尺寸与分辨率下仅具有线性容量。对于由经典标签选择的相位序列，量化了独立泡利噪声如何改变对数增强；在理想控制与校正后弱残余相位噪声条件下，针对固定小概率间隔证明了匹配的容量上下界，并指出残余相位翻转概率的倒数即为限制额外对数增长的相干时间尺度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使在内部记忆固定的情况下，量子器件的序贯响应容量仍随运行时长呈 K log K 的对数增强，而经典过程仅为线性增长；这种量子优势受到残余相位噪声的制约，其相干时间尺度由残余相位翻转概率的倒数决定。该工作为量子器件在噪声环境下长期运行的响应复杂度提供了严格的刻画框架。

Abstract: How complex can the responses of a quantum device become as it runs longer with a fixed internal memory? We quantify this complexity through sequential response capacity: how many adaptive testing stages, each using a fresh run, can continue to separate possible processes by a prescribed gap in response probabilities. For fixed system and memory sizes, we establish a tight law relating this capacity to run length and probability resolution. At fixed resolution, the capacity grows on the order of $K\log K$, where $K$ is the number of time steps in each run. Our construction attains this growth using time-dependent phase rotations on a single visible qubit with no additional internal memory; its tests give response probabilities exactly zero or one. Under the same tests, classical stochastic processes that measure in a fixed basis at every step have only linear capacity at fixed sizes and resolution. For phase sequences selected by a stored classical label, we then quantify how known independent Pauli noise changes this logarithmic enhancement. With ideal controls and weak residual phase noise after correction, we prove matching capacity bounds at a fixed small probability gap. These bounds identify the inverse residual phase-flip probability as the coherence timescale that limits the extra logarithmic growth.

</details>


### [98] [Universal Bound and Phase Transition in Many-Body Fermionic Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02075)
*Masahiro Hoshino,Ryota Matsuda,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以magic Rényi熵(MRE)度量费米子非高斯性，给出了其普适上界与Haar平均，并在SYK哈密顿量下虚时/实时演化中发现了MRE密度的一阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子非高斯性是通用量子计算的资源，可由量子多体相互作用产生，但如何定量刻画其增长并达到上界尚缺乏研究。

Method: 以MRE为非高斯性度量，推导其上界与Haar均值；在SYK哈密顿量下对高斯态做虚时与实时演化，通过改变两种演化时长扫描参数。

Result: 证明Haar随机态在大系统极限下达到每个Majorana为ln(4/3)的最大MRE密度；显示协方差矩阵相同的态其MRE可广延地不同；发现MRE密度出现kink的一阶相变，并伴随四个副本间置换对称性的自发破缺。

Conclusion: 该相变代表非高斯性的定性变化，且不反映在热自由能中，表明MRE捕捉到自由能无法探测的物理。

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity is a resource for universal quantum computation that can be generated by interactions in quantum many-body systems. Using the magic Rényi entropy (MRE) as a measure of non-Gaussianity, we derive its universal upper bound and Haar mean, proving that typical Haar-random states attain the maximal MRE density of $\ln(4/3)$ per Majorana in the large-system limit. We also show that the MRE can differ extensively between states with identical covariance matrices. To investigate how non-Gaussianity grows toward this maximal density, we evolve a Gaussian state in imaginary and real time under the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Hamiltonian. By varying the imaginary- and real-time durations, we identify a first-order transition marked by a kink in the MRE density and spontaneous breaking of the permutation symmetry among the four copies used to evaluate the MRE. This transition represents a qualitative change in the non-Gaussianity of the state that is not reflected in the thermal free energy.

</details>


### [99] [A computational phase diagram for the transverse field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02079)
*Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了横场Ising模型配分函数与Gibbs态观测量的经典可计算性，给出了阈值 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}\le 1$ 下的高效随机经典算法，并证明越过该阈值近似配分函数为NP难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横场Ising模型是量子多体物理中的基础模型，其配分函数与Gibbs态观测量的计算复杂度是量子复杂性理论的核心问题。作者希望刻画该模型在何种参数区间内可被经典高效近似，并确定其计算难度的相变边界。

Method: 针对哈密顿量 $H=\frac12\sum J_{ij}Z_iZ_j+\sum h_i^z Z_i+\eta\sum X_i$，引入相互作用矩阵谱宽 $\Delta(J)=\lambda_{\max}(J)-\lambda_{\min}(J)$。在条件 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}\le 1$ 下构造随机经典算法近似配分函数 $Z(\beta)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-\beta H})$ 及Gibbs态 $\rho_\beta$ 上Pauli弦观测量的期望值；在条件 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}>1$ 下用复杂性归约证明近似困难性。

Result: 当 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}\le 1$ 时，存在随机经典算法在关于 $n$、$\beta$、模型参数和 $\varepsilon^{-1}$ 的多项式时间内以相对误差 $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$ 近似 $Z(\beta)$；同一区间内还可对Pauli弦观测量实现任意小的加性误差近似，当观测量在 $X$ 基下对角（$P\in\{I,X\}^{\otimes n}$）时进一步达到任意小的相对误差。当 $\Delta(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(\beta\eta)}{\eta}>1$ 时，以 $\exp(o(n))$ 乘法因子近似 $Z(\beta)$ 是NP难的，在标准复杂性假设下不大可能存在高效经典或量子算法。

Conclusion: 该工作给出横场Ising模型经典可近似性的精确阈值判据：阈值以内配分函数与部分观测量的估计可用随机经典算法高效完成，阈值以外则计算难度急剧上升为NP难，从而划定了经典与量子计算能力的相变边界。

Abstract: We study the transverse field Ising model, defined by the Hamiltonian $H =\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i, j\in [n]} J_{ij} Z_i Z_j +\sum_{i=1}^n h_i^z Z_i + η\sum_{i} X_i$ where $J $ is the symmetric interaction matrix, and $η$ is the transverse field strength. Let $Δ(J)=λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)$ be the spectral width of $J.$ When the inverse temperature $β\geq0$ satisfies $Δ(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq1$, we give a randomized classical algorithm that approximates the partition function $Z(β)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})$ to a given relative error $ε\in(0,1)$ in time polynomial in $n$, $β$, the model parameters, and $ε^{-1}$. When $ Δ(J) \cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η > 1 ,$ we show that approximating $ Z(β)$ within an $\exp(o(n))$-multiplicative factor is $\textbf{NP}$-hard, and thus unlikely to admit an efficient classical or quantum algorithms under standard complexity theoretic assumptions. Furthermore, in the regime $Δ(J)\cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq 1,$ we provide an efficient randomized classical algorithm that approximates Pauli string observables of the Gibbs state $ ρ_β= \frac{e^{-βH}}{\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})}$ within an arbitrarily small additive error. In the special case when the observable is also diagonal in the $X$-basis, i.e. $P \in \{I, X\}^{\otimes n}$, the algorithm further achieves arbitrarily small relative error.

</details>


### [100] [Strong Simulation of 1D Quantum Circuits via Reduced Transition Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02082)
*Matilde Grassi,Stefano Carignano,Luca Tagliacozzo,Jacopo De Nardis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 Sweeping RTM 算法对 1D 混沌砖墙电路指定输出概率进行近似强模拟，数值证据表明达到固定相对精度所需键维随时间内亚指数增长。


<details>
